971  

972class Device: 

973 """Represent a GPU and act as an entry point for cuda.core features. 

974  

975 This is a singleton object that helps ensure interoperability 

976 across multiple libraries imported in the process to both see 

977 and use the same GPU device. 

978  

979 While acting as the entry point, many other CUDA resources can be 

980 allocated such as streams and buffers. Any :obj:`~_context.Context` dependent 

981 resource created through this device, will continue to refer to 

982 this device's context. 

983  

984 Newly returned :obj:`~_device.Device` objects are thread-local singletons 

985 for a specified device. 

986  

987 Note 

988 ---- 

989 Will not initialize the GPU. 

990  

991 Parameters 

992 ---------- 

993 device_id : int, optional 

994 Device ordinal to return a :obj:`~_device.Device` object for. 

995 Default value of `None` return the currently used device. 

996  

997 """ 

998 __slots__ = ( 

999 "_device_id", 

1000 "_memory_resource", 

1001 "_has_inited", 

1002 "_properties", 

1003 "_resources", 

1004 "_uuid", 

1005 "_context", 

1006 "__weakref__", 

1007 ) 

1008  

1009 def __new__(cls, device_id: Device | int | None = None) -> Device:

1074 def can_access_peer(self, peer: Device | int) -> bool: 

1075 """Check if this device can access memory from the specified peer device. 

1076  

1077 Queries whether peer-to-peer memory access is supported between this 

1078 device and the specified peer device. 

1079  

1080 Parameters 

1081 ---------- 

1082 peer : Device | int 

1083 The peer device to check accessibility to. Can be a :obj:`~_device.Device` object or device ID. 

1084 """ 

1085 peer = Device(peer) 

1086 cdef int d1 = <int> self.device_id 

1087 cdef int d2 = <int> peer.device_id 

1088 if d1 == d2: 

1089 return True 

1090 cdef int value = 0 

1091 with nogil: 

1092 HANDLE_RETURN(cydriver.cuDeviceCanAccessPeer(&value, d1, d2)) 

1093 return bool(value) 

1094

1249  

1250 def set_current(self, ctx: Context | None = None) -> Context | None: 

1251 """Set device to be used for GPU executions. 

1252  

1253 Initializes CUDA and sets the calling thread to a valid CUDA 

1254 context. By default the primary context is used, but optional `ctx` 

1255 parameter can be used to explicitly supply a :obj:`~_context.Context` object. 

1256  

1257 Providing a `ctx` causes the previous set context to be popped and returned. 

1258  

1259 If `ctx` was created on a different device than this receiver, the call 

1260 is delegated to that device's own :meth:`set_current`. This keeps the 

1261 owning device's bookkeeping consistent and lets a context this method 

1262 handed out for a foreign device be pushed back through any ``Device`` 

1263 object, matching the CUDA context stack's own thread-wide semantics. 

1264  

1265 Parameters 

1266 ---------- 

1267 ctx : :obj:`~_context.Context`, optional 

1268 Optional context to push onto this device's current thread stack. 

1269  

1270 Returns 

1271 ------- 

1272 :obj:`~_context.Context`, optional 

1273 The previous context, or ``None`` if no context was current. When 

1274 returned, its ``device_id`` identifies the device that was 

1275 previously current. 

1276  

1277 Examples 

1278 -------- 

1279 Acts as an entry point of this object. Users always start a code by 

1280 calling this method, e.g. 

1281  

1282 >>> from cuda.core import Device 

1283 >>> dev0 = Device(0) 

1284 >>> dev0.set_current() 

1285 >>> # ... do work on device 0 ... 

1286  

1287 """

1295 assert_type(ctx, Context) 2BHBI3nCIKEDIEI

1296 Context_check_open(ctx) 2BHBI3nCIKEDIEI

1297 if ctx._device_id != self._device_id: 2BHBI3nCIKEDI

1298 # The CUDA context stack is per-thread, not per-Device-object, 

1299 # so pushing/popping a foreign-device context is delegated to 

1300 # the device that owns it; its own bookkeeping (_context, 

1301 # _has_inited) is what should track this push, not ours. 

1302 return Device(ctx._device_id).set_current(ctx) 

1303 if self._has_inited and self._context is not None: 2BHBI3nCIKEDI

1304 prev_owned = self._context 2BHBI3nCIKEDI

1305 curr_ctx = as_cu(ctx._h_context) 2BHBI3nCIKEDI

1306 prev_ctx = NULL 2BHBI3nCIKEDI

1307 with nogil: 2BHBI3nCIKEDI

1308 HANDLE_RETURN(cydriver.cuCtxGetCurrent(&prev_ctx)) 2BHBI3nCIKEDI

1309 if prev_ctx != NULL: 2BHBI3nCIKEDI

1310 HANDLE_RETURN(cydriver.cuCtxGetDevice(&prev_dev)) 2BI3nCIKEDI

1311 # cuCtxSetCurrent replaces the top of the thread's context stack 

1312 # in one driver call (or binds ctx when nothing is current), so 

1313 # a failure leaves the previous context current instead of 

1314 # leaving the thread with no context, as a failed pop-then-push 

1315 # would. 

1316 HANDLE_RETURN(cydriver.cuCtxSetCurrent(curr_ctx)) 2BHBI3nCIKEDI

1317 self._has_inited = True 2BHBI3nCIKEDI

1318 self._context = ctx # Store owning context reference 2BHBI3nCIKEDI

1319 if prev_ctx != NULL: 2BHBI3nCIKEDI

1320 if prev_owned is not None and as_cu(prev_owned._h_context) == prev_ctx: 2BI3nCIKEDI

1321 return prev_owned 2BI3nCIKEDI

1322 return Context._from_handle( 23n

1323 Context, create_context_handle_ref(prev_ctx), <int>prev_dev) 23n

1403 def create_stream(self, obj: IsStreamType | None = None, options: StreamOptions | None = None) -> Stream: 

1404 """Create or wrap a :obj:`~_stream.Stream` object. 

1405  

1406 New stream objects can be created in two different ways: 

1407  

1408 1) Create a new CUDA stream with customizable ``options``.

1410  

1411 Option (2) internally holds a reference to the foreign object 

1412 such that the lifetime is managed.
